Second, we present MiRA (Milestoning your Reinforcement Learning Enhanced Agent), an RL training framework that uses dense, milestone-based reward signals. The real-time planning mechanism improves proprietary models such as Gemini by approximately a 10% absolute increase in success rate (SR) on the WebArena-Lite benchmark. Meanwhile, applying MiRA to the open Gemma3-12B model increases its success rate from 6.4% to 43.0%. This performance surpasses proprietary systems such as GPT-4-Turbo (17.6%) and GPT-4o (13.9%), as well as the previous open-model state of the art, WebRL (38.4%). Overall, our findings demonstrate that combining explicit inference-time planning with milestone-based rewards significantly improves an agent's long-horizon capabilities, paving the way for more robust and general-purpose autonomous systems.

</details>


### [77] [Stepwise: Neuro-Symbolic Proof Search for Automated Systems Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19715)
*Baoding He,Zenan Li,Wei Sun,Yuan Yao,Taolue Chen,Xiaoxing Ma,Zhendong Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号证明生成框架，通过结合LLM的推理能力和ITP工具的符号验证，自动化系统级验证项目的证明搜索，显著提升了定理证明的效率和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化验证通过交互式定理证明对关键系统进行正确性验证，但大型证明脚本的构建高度手动且可扩展性有限。LLM在数学推理方面的进步使其在软件验证中的集成前景广阔。

Method: 提出神经符号证明生成框架：1) 在证明状态上进行最佳优先树搜索；2) 反复查询LLM获取候选证明步骤；3) 神经侧：使用证明状态-步骤对数据集微调LLM；4) 符号侧：集成ITP工具修复被拒绝的步骤、过滤和排序证明状态、在搜索停滞时自动解除子目标；5) 实现新的Isabelle REPL，暴露细粒度证明状态和自动化工具。

Result: 在seL4基准测试中，系统证明了高达77.6%的定理，显著超过之前的LLM方法和独立Sledgehammer，解决了更多多步证明。在其他基准测试中也展示了强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该框架通过神经符号协同实现了数据高效的LLM适应和语义感知的搜索空间剪枝，为可扩展的自动化软件验证提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Formal verification via interactive theorem proving is increasingly used to ensure the correctness of critical systems, yet constructing large proof scripts remains highly manual and limits scalability. Advances in large language models (LLMs), especially in mathematical reasoning, make their integration into software verification increasingly promising. This paper introduces a neuro-symbolic proof generation framework designed to automate proof search for systems-level verification projects. The framework performs a best-first tree search over proof states, repeatedly querying an LLM for the next candidate proof step. On the neural side, we fine-tune LLMs using datasets of proof state-step pairs; on the symbolic side, we incorporate a range of ITP tools to repair rejected steps, filter and rank proof states, and automatically discharge subgoals when search progress stalls. This synergy enables data-efficient LLM adaptation and semantics-informed pruning of the search space. We implement the framework on a new Isabelle REPL that exposes fine-grained proof states and automation tools, and evaluate it on the FVEL seL4 benchmark and additional Isabelle developments. On seL4, the system proves up to 77.6\% of the theorems, substantially surpassing previous LLM-based approaches and standalone Sledgehammer, while solving significantly more multi-step proofs. Results across further benchmarks demonstrate strong generalization, indicating a viable path toward scalable automated software verification.

</details>


### [78] [Embodied Science: Closing the Discovery Loop with Agentic Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19782)
*Xiang Zhuang,Chenyi Zhou,Kehua Feng,Zhihui Zhu,Yunfan Gao,Yijie Zhong,Yichi Zhang,Junjie Huang,Keyan Ding,Lei Bai,Haofen Wang,Qiang Zhang,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"具身科学"范式，将科学发现重构为智能体推理与物理执行的闭环耦合，通过PLAD框架实现自主发现系统


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI在预测科学属性方面表现出色，但科学发现本质上是物理的、长周期的实验循环过程。现有计算方法大多与这一现实脱节，将发现视为孤立的、特定任务的预测，而非与物理世界的持续交互。

Method: 提出具身科学范式，构建统一的感知-语言-行动-发现(PLAD)框架。该框架中，具身智能体感知实验环境，基于科学知识进行推理，执行物理干预，并将结果内化以驱动后续探索。

Result: 通过将计算推理建立在稳健的物理反馈基础上，该方法弥合了数字预测与实证验证之间的差距。

Conclusion: 具身科学范式为生命科学和化学科学中的自主发现系统提供了路线图，通过智能体推理与物理执行的紧密耦合，实现真正的科学发现闭环。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence has demonstrated remarkable capability in predicting scientific properties, yet scientific discovery remains an inherently physical, long-horizon pursuit governed by experimental cycles. Most current computational approaches are misaligned with this reality, framing discovery as isolated, task-specific predictions rather than continuous interaction with the physical world. Here, we argue for embodied science, a paradigm that reframes scientific discovery as a closed loop tightly coupling agentic reasoning with physical execution. We propose a unified Perception-Language-Action-Discovery (PLAD) framework, wherein embodied agents perceive experimental environments, reason over scientific knowledge, execute physical interventions, and internalize outcomes to drive subsequent exploration. By grounding computational reasoning in robust physical feedback, this approach bridges the gap between digital prediction and empirical validation, offering a roadmap for autonomous discovery systems in the life and chemical sciences.

</details>


### [79] [FormalEvolve: Neuro-Symbolic Evolutionary Search for Diverse and Prover-Effective Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19828)
*Haijian Lu,Wei Wang,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FormalEvolve：一种编译门控的神经符号进化框架，通过LLM驱动的变异和交叉生成多样化候选形式化，结合AST重写操作提升结构多样性，在有限预算下提高语义命中率和下游证明性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动形式化方法虽然能保证语义一致性，但忽略了证明搜索成本和成功率。语义一致的形式化在证明搜索成本和成功率上可能存在显著差异，需要优化形式化过程以提高下游证明性能。

Method: 提出FormalEvolve框架：1) 将自动形式化建模为有预算的测试时搜索问题；2) 使用LLM驱动的变异和交叉生成多样化候选；3) 结合有界补丁修复；4) 引入符号AST重写操作增加结构多样性；5) 采用编译门控机制筛选有效候选。

Result: 在CombiBench和ProofNet数据集上，T=100的生成器调用预算下，FormalEvolve达到58.0%和84.9%的语义命中率(SH@100)，降低了语义成功率的跨问题集中度(Gini系数更低)。在固定证明器预算下，也提升了CombiBench的下游证明性能。

Conclusion: FormalEvolve通过神经符号进化方法有效解决了自动形式化中的证明效率问题，在有限预算下生成多样化且证明友好的形式化，显著提高了语义命中率和下游证明成功率。

Abstract: Autoformalization aims to translate natural-language mathematics into compilable, machine-checkable statements. However, semantic consistency does not imply prover effectiveness: even semantically consistent formalizations can differ substantially in proof-search cost and success rate. In this work, we formulate autoformalization as a budgeted, test-time search for semantically consistent repertoires, and propose FormalEvolve, a compilation-gated neuro-symbolic evolutionary framework. FormalEvolve generates diverse candidates via LLM-driven mutation and crossover with bounded patch repair, while symbolic Abstract Syntax Tree (AST) rewrite operations further inject structural diversity. On CombiBench and ProofNet, under a strict generator-call budget of T = 100, FormalEvolve reaches semantic hit rates (SH@100) of 58.0% and 84.9%, and reduces cross-problem concentration of semantic successes(lower Gini). Under a fixed prover budget, FormalEvolve also improves downstream proving performance on CombiBench. Code will be released publicly.

</details>


### [80] [On the Ability of Transformers to Verify Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19954)
*Yash Sarrof,Yupei Du,Katharina Stein,Alexander Koller,Sylvie Thiébaux,Michael Hahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了Transformer在AI规划任务中的泛化能力，特别关注解码器模型验证给定规划是否正确的理论保证，提出了C*-RASP框架来处理序列长度和词汇量同时增长的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer在AI规划任务中的成功表现不一致，且对其泛化能力的理论理解有限。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，分析解码器模型在验证规划正确性时的能力，特别是在对象数量增长导致输入字母表扩展的测试场景下。

Method: 提出了C*-RASP框架，这是C-RASP的扩展，用于在序列长度和词汇量同时增长的情况下建立Transformer的长度泛化保证。通过理论分析识别了经典规划领域中Transformer可学习验证长规划的大类，并研究了影响长度可泛化解可学习性的结构特性。

Result: 理论分析识别了Transformer可证明学习验证长规划的大类经典规划领域，并揭示了显著影响长度可泛化解可学习性的结构特性。实证实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 本文在理解Transformer在AI规划任务中的泛化能力方面迈出了重要一步，提出了理论框架来分析解码器模型验证规划的能力，特别是在对象数量增长的情况下，为Transformer在规划任务中的可学习性提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Transformers have shown inconsistent success in AI planning tasks, and theoretical understanding of when generalization should be expected has been limited. We take important steps towards addressing this gap by analyzing the ability of decoder-only models to verify whether a given plan correctly solves a given planning instance. To analyse the general setting where the number of objects -- and thus the effective input alphabet -- grows at test time, we introduce C*-RASP, an extension of C-RASP designed to establish length generalization guarantees for transformers under the simultaneous growth in sequence length and vocabulary size. Our results identify a large class of classical planning domains for which transformers can provably learn to verify long plans, and structural properties that significantly affects the learnability of length generalizable solutions. Empirical experiments corroborate our theory.

</details>


### [81] [Experience is the Best Teacher: Motivating Effective Exploration in Reinforcement Learning for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20046)
*Wenjian Zhang,Kongcheng Zhang,Jiaxin Qi,Baisheng Lai,Jianqiang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HeRL提出了一种基于后见经验引导的强化学习框架，通过利用失败轨迹和未满足的评分标准作为指导，引导LLM探索超出当前策略分布的理想行为，从而提高推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于评分标准的强化学习虽然提升了LLM的通用推理能力，但存在探索效率低的问题，探索局限于当前策略分布。RL优化本质上是将策略导向最大化奖励的理想分布，而有效探索应与期望目标对齐。

Method: HeRL将失败轨迹及其未满足的评分标准作为后见经验，作为上下文指导引导策略探索超出当前分布的期望响应。同时引入奖励加成激励在指导下具有更大改进潜力的响应，避免从头开始的重复试错。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的广泛实验表明，HeRL相比基线方法获得了更优越的性能提升，并且能够在测试时进一步受益于经验引导的自我改进。

Conclusion: HeRL通过后见经验引导有效探索，从高质量样本中学习而不需要从头重复试错，理论上提供了更准确的期望梯度估计，显著提升了强化学习在LLM推理任务中的效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) with rubric-based rewards has recently shown remarkable progress in enhancing general reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), yet still suffers from ineffective exploration confined to curent policy distribution. In fact, RL optimization can be viewed as steering the policy toward an ideal distribution that maximizes the rewards, while effective exploration should align efforts with desired target. Leveraging this insight, we propose HeRL, a Hindsight experience guided Reinforcement Learning framework to bootstrap effective exploration by explicitly telling LLMs the desired behaviors specified in rewards. Concretely, HeRL treats failed trajectories along with their unmet rubrics as hindsight experience, which serves as in-context guidance for the policy to explore desired responses beyond its current distribution. Additionally, we introduce a bonus reward to incentivize responses with greater potential for improvement under such guidance. HeRL facilitates effective learning from desired high quality samples without repeated trial-and-error from scratch, yielding a more accurate estimation of the expected gradient theoretically. Extensive experiments across various benchmarks demonstrate that HeRL achieves superior performance gains over baselines, and can further benefit from experience guided self-improvement at test time. Our code is available at https://github.com/sikelifei/HeRL.

</details>


### [82] [DIAL-KG: Schema-Free Incremental Knowledge Graph Construction via Dynamic Schema Induction and Evolution-Intent Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20059)
*Weidong Bao,Yilin Wang,Ruyu Gao,Fangling Leng,Yubin Bao,Ge Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DIAL-KG：基于元知识库的增量知识图谱构建闭环框架，解决传统静态构建方法的局限性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统知识图谱构建方法存在两大问题：1）静态构建，需要从固定语料库一次性构建，无法适应动态数据环境；2）依赖预定义模式，缺乏灵活性。当新数据到达时，需要完全重建图谱，计算成本高昂。

Method: 提出DIAL-KG闭环框架，由元知识库协调，包含三阶段循环：1）双轨提取：默认三元组生成确保知识完整性，复杂知识切换为事件提取；2）治理裁决：确保提取事实的保真度和时效性，防止幻觉和知识陈旧；3）模式演化：从验证知识中归纳新模式指导后续构建，并将当前轮次知识增量应用到现有图谱。

Result: 大量实验表明，该框架在构建图谱质量和归纳模式质量方面均达到最先进的性能水平。

Conclusion: DIAL-KG框架有效解决了传统知识图谱构建方法的局限性，通过增量构建和模式演化机制，能够适应动态数据环境，提高构建效率和灵活性。

Abstract: Knowledge Graphs (KGs) are foundational to applications such as search, question answering, and recommendation. Conventional knowledge graph construction methods are predominantly static, rely ing on a single-step construction from a fixed corpus with a prede f
ined schema. However, such methods are suboptimal for real-world sce narios where data arrives dynamically, as incorporating new informa tion requires complete and computationally expensive graph reconstruc tions. Furthermore, predefined schemas hinder the flexibility of knowl edge graph construction. To address these limitations, we introduce DIAL KG, a closed-loop framework for incremental KG construction orches trated by a Meta-Knowledge Base (MKB). The framework oper ates in a three-stage cycle: (i) Dual-Track Extraction, which ensures knowledge completeness by defaulting to triple generation and switching to event extraction for complex knowledge; (ii) Governance Adjudica tion, which ensures the fidelity and currency of extracted facts to prevent hallucinations and knowledge staleness; and (iii) Schema Evolution, in which new schemas are induced from validated knowledge to guide subsequent construction cycles, and knowledge from the current round is incrementally applied to the existing KG. Extensive experiments demon strate that our framework achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance in the quality of both the constructed graph and the induced schemas.

</details>


### [83] [Learning Dynamic Belief Graphs for Theory-of-mind Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20170)
*Ruxiao Chen,Xilei Zhao,Thomas J. Cova,Frank A. Drews,Susu Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于大语言模型的结构化认知轨迹模型，用于心理理论推理，通过动态信念图表示心理状态，显著提升了动态情境下的行动预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型心理理论推理方法要么直接提示，要么使用将信念视为静态独立的潜状态模型，导致随时间推移产生不连贯的心理模型，在动态情境下推理能力较弱。特别是在灾难响应、紧急医疗等高风险环境中，需要准确推断人们如何基于隐含的、演变的信念来寻求信息和采取行动。

Method: 引入结构化认知轨迹模型，将心理状态表示为动态信念图，联合推断潜信念、学习其随时间变化的依赖关系，并将信念演化与信息寻求和决策联系起来。具体包括：(1) 从文本化概率陈述到一致概率图模型更新的新颖投影；(2) 基于能量的因子图表示信念相互依赖关系；(3) 捕捉信念积累和延迟决策的基于ELBO的目标函数。

Result: 在多个真实世界灾难疏散数据集上，该模型显著改善了行动预测，并恢复了与人类推理一致的可解释信念轨迹，为在高不确定性环境中增强大语言模型的心理理论推理能力提供了原则性模块。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种结构化的认知轨迹模型，能够有效表示动态心理状态并推断信念演化，为高风险环境中的大语言模型心理理论推理提供了更强大、更连贯的方法，在行动预测和信念轨迹解释方面取得了显著改进。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM) reasoning with Large Language Models (LLMs) requires inferring how people's implicit, evolving beliefs shape what they seek and how they act under uncertainty -- especially in high-stakes settings such as disaster response, emergency medicine, and human-in-the-loop autonomy. Prior approaches either prompt LLMs directly or use latent-state models that treat beliefs as static and independent, often producing incoherent mental models over time and weak reasoning in dynamic contexts. We introduce a structured cognitive trajectory model for LLM-based ToM that represents mental state as a dynamic belief graph, jointly inferring latent beliefs, learning their time-varying dependencies, and linking belief evolution to information seeking and decisions. Our model contributes (i) a novel projection from textualized probabilistic statements to consistent probabilistic graphical model updates, (ii) an energy-based factor graph representation of belief interdependencies, and (iii) an ELBO-based objective that captures belief accumulation and delayed decisions. Across multiple real-world disaster evacuation datasets, our model significantly improves action prediction and recovers interpretable belief trajectories consistent with human reasoning, providing a principled module for augmenting LLMs with ToM in high-uncertainty environment. https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ICML_submission-6373/

</details>


### [84] [Survey of Various Fuzzy and Uncertain Decision-Making Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15709)
*Takaaki Fujita,Florentin Smarandache*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文是一篇关于不确定性感知多准则决策（MCDM）的综述，提出了任务导向的分类法，总结了问题设置、权重确定、准则结构建模等方法，并提供了方法选择指南和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实决策常受模糊性、信息不完整、异构数据和专家意见冲突等因素影响，需要系统梳理不确定性感知MCDM方法，为研究人员和实践者提供清晰的分类框架和方法选择指导。

Method: 采用综述研究方法，构建任务导向的分类法，从问题层面设置（离散、群体/共识、动态等）、权重确定（主观/客观方案）、准则间结构和因果关系建模等方面组织现有方法，对比补偿性评分方法、距离参考和折衷方法、非补偿性排序框架等解决方案。

Result: 提出了一个简洁的任务导向分类法，系统总结了不确定性感知MCDM领域的主要方法、典型输入、核心计算步骤和主要输出，提供了根据鲁棒性、可解释性和数据可用性选择方法的指导原则。

Conclusion: 该综述为不确定性感知MCDM提供了系统框架，指出了未来研究方向，包括可解释的不确定性集成、稳定性分析，以及大规模动态决策环境中的可扩展性问题。

Abstract: Decision-making in real applications is often affected by vagueness, incomplete information, heterogeneous data, and conflicting expert opinions. This survey reviews uncertainty-aware multi-criteria decision-making (MCDM) and organizes the field into a concise, task-oriented taxonomy. We summarize problem-level settings (discrete, group/consensus, dynamic, multi-stage, multi-level, multiagent, and multi-scenario), weight elicitation (subjective and objective schemes under fuzzy/linguistic inputs), and inter-criteria structure and causality modelling. For solution procedures, we contrast compensatory scoring methods, distance-to-reference and compromise approaches, and non-compensatory outranking frameworks for ranking or sorting. We also outline rule/evidence-based and sequential decision models that produce interpretable rules or policies. The survey highlights typical inputs, core computational steps, and primary outputs, and provides guidance on choosing methods according to robustness, interpretability, and data availability. It concludes with open directions on explainable uncertainty integration, stability, and scalability in large-scale and dynamic decision environments.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [85] [Leveraging Classical and Quantum Computing for Process Systems Engineering Applications: Decomposition Algorithm with Ising Solvers for Efficient Discrete Landscape Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19520)
*Yirang Park,David E. Bernal Neira*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该研究评估了基于伊辛模型的求解器在化学工程概念过程设计中的离散优化应用，通过两个案例研究比较了经典求解器与启发式方法（模拟退火、量子退火、熵计算）的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学工程概念过程设计中的混合整数非线性规划问题具有组合复杂性和非线性特征，传统方法面临挑战。研究旨在探索利用伊辛求解器处理离散子问题，结合模拟器和非线性求解器处理非线性部分，构建异构计算框架来解决实际优化问题。

Method: 采用分解方法将整体问题分解为离散子问题和非线性部分。离散子问题被表述为整数规划或二次无约束二进制优化问题，使用商业经典求解器和三种伊辛求解器（模拟退火、量子退火、熵计算）进行求解。通过两个案例研究进行评估：离子液体选择及其过程设计，以及更复杂的药物制造过程优化。

Result: 商业经典求解器运行时间最短，熵计算耗时最长，量子退火和模拟退火次之。启发式方法在单次运行中识别出所有或大部分可行解，在解多样性和高效广泛探索解空间方面优于经典求解器，而经典求解器提供最优性保证和快速收敛速度。

Conclusion: 在过程设计中，替代设计的洞察和成本比较比最优解更有价值，启发式方法提供了更合适的决策策略。异构计算方法结合不同方法的优势，为解决实际优化问题提供了有前景的途径。

Abstract: Conceptual process design is a crucial aspect of chemical engineering that involves process synthesis. Mixed-integer nonlinear programming is a powerful framework for modeling such design problems by combining discrete and continuous variables; however, the combinatorial complexity of discrete choices, coupled with nonlinearities, presents challenging monolithic problems. Using decomposition, discrete subproblems can potentially benefit from Ising solvers, while simulators and nonlinear solvers offer powerful tools for handling nonlinearities. This work aims to: evaluate use of Ising-based solvers for discrete optimization and holistic process optimization through two case studies: an ionic liquid selection and its process design, and a more complex problem of drug manufacturing process optimization. The discrete subproblem is formulated as an integer program or quadratic unconstrained binary optimization and solved using a commercial classical or Ising solvers such as simulated annealing (SA), quantum annealing (QA), and entropy computing (EC) respectively. The commercial classical solver had the shortest runtime, whereas EC took the longest, followed by QA and SA, in reaching feasible and optimal solutions. The heuristics identified all or most feasible solutions in a single run, demonstrating advantages in solution diversity and efficient and broad exploration of the solution space over the classical solver, while the classical solver provides an optimality guarantee and rapid convergence speed. In process design, where insights of alternative designs and cost comparisons are more valuable than an optimal solution, heuristics offer a better-suited decision-making strategy. The comparative analysis highlights the strengths of each method and underscores the potential of this heterogeneous computing approach that leverages different methods to address practical optimization problems.

</details>


### [86] [On Locational Marginal Emissions in Electricity Markets: A Two-Layered Dispatch Mechanism and Its Fundamental Theorems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19530)
*Luc Cote,Andy Sun*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种双层调度机制的电力市场设计，将碳核算系统性地纳入电网运营，通过定义位置边际排放量（LMEs）实现经济效率与减排目标的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电力市场设计主要关注经济效率，缺乏系统性的碳核算机制。需要一种既能保持经济效率又能有效减少碳排放的市场框架，将碳核算整合到电网运营中。

Method: 采用双层调度机制：第一层最小化财务成本以保持经济效率；第二层在成本最优解集合内最小化系统排放。定义位置边际排放量（LMEs）作为双层规划对偶变量导出的系统排放边际率。

Result: 证明了LMEs满足福利经济学第一和第二基本定理的类似性质：(1)个体电网实体的分散化"碳利润"最大化保证了与经济调度一致的系统排放；(2)任何最优低碳经济调度都可由相应的LME信号支持作为分散均衡。建立了碳足迹定理，确保所有电网组件的碳账户总和等于实际物理碳排放。

Conclusion: 该研究为位置边际排放量（LMEs）建立了完整的理论基础，通过双层调度机制成功将碳核算系统性地整合到电力市场设计中，实现了经济效率与减排目标的统一，并通过德州电网模型验证了其经验性质。

Abstract: We propose a market design for real-time electricity markets that utilizes a two-layered dispatch mechanism to systematically incorporate carbon accounting into grid operations. In this mechanism, ``dispatch'', the centralized allocation of generation resources to meet system load, is executed via a hierarchical structure where the first layer minimizes financial costs to maintain economic efficiency, while the second layer minimizes system emissions strictly within the set of cost-optimal solutions. We define locational marginal emissions (LMEs) as the marginal rate of system emissions derived from the dual variables of the two-layered formulation. Unlike standard marginal prices which correspond to right-hand-side constraint relaxations, LMEs must account for the requirement of economic optimality which introduces demand parameters into the problem's constraint structure. Under the framework, we establish that LMEs satisfy properties analogous to the first and second fundamental theorems of welfare economics. We prove that (1) decentralized ``carbon profit'' maximization by individual grid entities guarantees a system-wide emission profile consistent with the economic dispatch, and (2) any optimal low-carbon economic dispatch is supported by a corresponding set of LME signals acting as a decentralized equilibrium. Furthermore, we establish a general carbon accounting theorem, called the Carbon Footprint Theorem, showing that these market-consistent LMEs ensure the sum of carbon accounts across all grid components (loads, generators, transmission, and storage) equals the total physical carbon emissions. This completes the theoretical foundation of the LME. Finally, we investigate and validate the empirical properties of LMEs and LME-based carbon accounting through case studies on a realistic Texas grid model.

</details>


### [87] [Games with Incomplete Information Played by Risk-Revising Players](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19738)
*Shutian Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文将风险修正玩家引入不完全信息博弈，提出了基于相干风险度量的风险厌恶均衡概念，包括事前风险厌恶纳什均衡和事中风险修正贝叶斯纳什均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究不完全信息博弈中玩家的风险偏好如何影响均衡结果，特别是当玩家根据私有信息修正其风险偏好时的行为后果。

Method: 引入具有相干风险度量的风险修正玩家，扩展标准纳什均衡和贝叶斯纳什均衡为风险厌恶版本，提出风险修正贝叶斯纳什均衡，利用风险度量的对偶表示分析风险厌恶的作用。

Result: 建立了这些均衡概念的存在性结果，当玩家的风险修正对应于其事前均衡行为时，建立了事前和事中阶段均衡之间的联系，通过比较静态分析风险厌恶效应。

Conclusion: 风险修正框架为分析不完全信息博弈中的风险厌恶行为提供了系统方法，风险度量的对偶表示有助于理解不一致信念的表示，数值示例展示了特定风险偏好下的均衡概念。

Abstract: This paper introduces risk-revising players to a class of games with incomplete information. These players enter the game with ex ante risk preferences represented by coherent risk measures and develop time-consistent interim revisions of them contingent on their private information. The standard Nash equilibrium at ex ante stage and Bayesian Nash equilibrium at interim stage are extended to their risk-averse counterparts. Risk-revising Bayesian Nash equilibrium is proposed to capture behavioral outcomes resulting from interim plays based on revised risk preferences. We discuss existence results of these equilibrium concepts. When players' risk revisions correspond to their ex ante equilibrium play, connections are established between equilibria at the ex ante and interim stages. The effect of risk-aversion is analyzed using comparative statics. With the help of the dual representation of risk measures, we illustrate the role of risk-aversion in representing inconsistent beliefs. A numerical example is presented to illustrate the proposed equilibrium concepts under a specific choice of risk preference.

</details>


### [88] [The incomplete Traveling Tournament Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19754)
*Karel Devriesere,David Van Bulck,Dries Goossens*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了不完全旅行锦标赛问题，将经典旅行锦标赛问题扩展到不完全循环赛制中，证明了该问题的NP难解性，并提出了多种求解方法和实例分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的旅行锦标赛问题扩展到不完全循环赛制中，研究每支队伍最多与对手比赛一次的情况，填补了该领域的研究空白。

Method: 1. 证明问题的NP难解性；2. 提出并比较两种整数规划模型及其线性松弛；3. 提出第三种基于固定主客场模式的模型；4. 针对该问题定制元启发式算法，并提出新的邻域结构；5. 设计问题实例并推导上下界。

Result: 1. 证明了不完全旅行锦标赛问题是NP难的；2. 提出的邻域结构能够完全连接主客场模式解空间；3. 设计的问题实例具有挑战性，为未来算法开发提供了基准。

Conclusion: 不完全旅行锦标赛问题是一个具有挑战性的NP难问题，提出的模型和算法为后续研究奠定了基础，开发高效算法是该领域未来的重要研究方向。

Abstract: We present a new problem called the incomplete Traveling Tournament problem, which introduces the well known Traveling Tournament Problem into the realm of incomplete round-robin tournaments. We focus on the case where teams can face each opponent at most once. We give a formal description of this problem and show that it is NP-hard. We first discuss how we can obtain lower bounds and how to strengthen them. Then, we propose two integer programming formulations and compare their LP-relaxations. We also propose a third formulation that assumes that home-away patterns of teams are fixed. We discuss how a recently proposed metaheuristic for incomplete round-robin scheduling can be tailored to our problem. In doing so, we present a novel neighborhood structure and show it fully connects the home-away pattern solution space. Finally, problem instances are proposed, for which we derive lower and upper bounds. We show that these instances are challenging, making the development of efficient algorithms for the incomplete Traveling Tournament problem an interesting direction for future research.

</details>


### [89] [Infinite-dimensional spherical-radial decomposition for probabilistic functions, with application to constrained optimal control and Gaussian process regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19907)
*Kewei Wang,Georg Stadler*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出hiSRD方法，将球面径向分解推广到无限随机维度，结合子空间SRD与蒙特卡洛方法，为机会约束优化提供无偏低方差估计器


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统球面径向分解仅适用于有限维椭圆分布，需要将其推广到无限随机维度以处理更复杂的概率函数估计问题，特别是在机会约束优化中

Method: 将子空间SRD与标准蒙特卡洛方法结合，提出混合无限维SRD方法，消除截断引起的偏差，降低方差，并支持概率函数导数的计算

Result: hiSRD提供无偏、低方差的估计器，理论分析显示能消除截断偏差并降低方差，数值研究验证了方法在随机PDE最优控制和GPR核参数优化中的有效性

Conclusion: hiSRD成功将SRD推广到无限维，为机会约束优化问题提供了有效的概率函数估计方法，在理论和数值上均表现出优越性能

Abstract: The spherical-radial decomposition (SRD) is an efficient method for estimating probabilistic functions and their gradients defined over finite-dimensional elliptical distributions. In this work, we generalize the SRD to infinite stochastic dimensions by combining subspace SRD with standard Monte Carlo methods. The resulting method, which we call hybrid infinite-dimensional SRD (hiSRD) provides an unbiased, low-variance estimator for convex sets arising, for instance, in chance-constrained optimization. We provide a theoretical analysis of the variance of finite-dimensional SRD as the dimension increases, and show that the proposed hybrid method eliminates truncation-induced bias, reduces variance, and allows the computation of derivatives of probabilistic functions. We present comprehensive numerical studies for a risk-neutral stochastic PDE optimal control problem with joint chance state constraints, and for optimizing kernel parameters in Gaussian process regression under the constraint that the posterior process satisfies joint chance constraints.

</details>


### [90] [Interpreting Reinforcement Learning Model Behavior via Koopman with Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19968)
*William T. Redman*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出使用带控制的Koopman算子框架来分析强化学习模型的行为，通过数据驱动方法近似Koopman算子来获取RL模型的动力学信息，从而增强可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习模型能够学习复杂行为，但定量评估这些行为（对安全保证和新策略发现至关重要）具有挑战性。研究者假设将RL模型视为控制系统，其关联的Koopman算子的数据驱动近似可能提供关于其行为的动力学信息，从而实现更好的可解释性。

Method: 应用带控制的Koopman框架到在多个标准基准环境中训练的RL模型上，通过数据驱动方法近似Koopman算子，分析拟合线性控制模型的稳定性、可控性等性质在训练过程中的演化。

Result: 研究发现拟合线性控制模型的稳定性、可控性等性质在训练过程中以任务依赖的方式演化。即使RL模型获得的奖励保持静态时，稳定性和可控性仍在演化，并能预测进一步训练后的奖励提升。这些指标可作为隐藏的进度度量，比较不同训练时期或不同优化RL模型的这些指标可以理解它们的差异。

Conclusion: 带控制的Koopman框架为分析和解释RL模型行为（特别是在训练过程中）提供了一种全面的方法，这些指标可能作为机械可解释性中的隐藏进度度量，有助于理解RL模型的内部动力学演化。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) models have shown the capability of learning complex behaviors, but quantitatively assessing those behaviors - which is critical for safety assurance and the discovery of novel strategies - is challenging. By viewing RL models as control systems, we hypothesize that data-driven approximations of their associated Koopman operators may provide dynamical information about their behavior, thus enabling greater interpretability. To test this, we apply the Koopman with control framework to RL models trained on several standard benchmark environments and demonstrate that properties of the fit linear control models, such as stability and controllability, evolve during training in a task dependent manner. Comparing these metrics across different training epochs or across differently optimized RL models enables an understanding of how they differ. In addition, we find cases where - even when the reward achieved by the RL model is static - the stability and controllability is nonetheless evolving, predicting increased reward with further training. This suggests that these metrics may be able to serve as hidden progress measures, a core idea in mechanistic interpretability. Taken together, our results illustrate that the Koopman with control framework provides a comprehensive way in which to analyze and interpret the behavior of RL models, particularly across training.

</details>


### [91] [Constrained Nonnegative Gram Feasibility is $\exists\mathbb{R}$-Complete](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19976)
*Angshul Majumdar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 约束非负Gram可行性问题是∃ℝ完全的，即使在秩r=2时也成立，通过从ETR-AMI问题的多项式时间归约证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究约束非负Gram可行性问题的计算复杂性，该问题在结构化低秩矩阵表示和几何嵌入问题中自然出现，旨在确定其计算复杂度类别。

Method: 通过从算术可行性问题ETR-AMI的多项式时间归约证明∃ℝ完全性。归约利用几何编码：在ℝ⁺²中固定锚方向，用形式为(x,1)的向量表示变量，通过内积关系实现加法和乘法约束。

Result: 证明约束非负Gram可行性问题是∃ℝ完全的，即使在秩r=2时也成立。硬度结果扩展到所有固定秩r≥2的情况。建立了该问题属于存在实数理论完全问题家族。

Conclusion: 约束对称非负Gram分解属于几何可行性问题中∃ℝ完全问题家族。结果揭示了该问题的计算难度，但未约束对称非负分解可行性的复杂度仍为开放问题。

Abstract: We study the computational complexity of constrained nonnegative Gram feasibility. Given a partially specified symmetric matrix together with affine relations among selected entries, the problem asks whether there exists a nonnegative matrix $H \in \mathbb{R}_+^{n\times r}$ such that $W = HH^\top$ satisfies all specified entries and affine constraints. Such factorizations arise naturally in structured low-rank matrix representations and geometric embedding problems.
  We prove that this feasibility problem is $\exists\mathbb{R}$-complete already for rank $r=2$. The hardness result is obtained via a polynomial-time reduction from the arithmetic feasibility problem \textsc{ETR-AMI}. The reduction exploits a geometric encoding of arithmetic constraints within rank-$2$ nonnegative Gram representations: by fixing anchor directions in $\mathbb{R}_+^2$ and representing variables through vectors of the form $(x,1)$, addition and multiplication constraints can be realized through inner-product relations.
  Combined with the semialgebraic formulation of the feasibility conditions, this establishes $\exists\mathbb{R}$-completeness. We further show that the hardness extends to every fixed rank $r\ge 2$. Our results place constrained symmetric nonnegative Gram factorization among the growing family of geometric feasibility problems that are complete for the existential theory of the reals.
  Finally, we discuss limitations of the result and highlight the open problem of determining the complexity of unconstrained symmetric nonnegative factorization feasibility.

</details>


### [92] [Reducing the Incentive to Tank: The Ex Post Gold Plan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20097)
*Bret Benesh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文提出REWIND指标和Ex Post Gold方案，通过引入事后淘汰日期的不确定性来减少NBA选秀抽签中的摆烂行为，激励球队在整个赛季保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有减少选秀抽签摆烂的方案存在可预测的转折点，球队可以战略性地利用这些时间点进行摆烂。需要设计一种机制来削弱战略性输球的激励，同时保持淘汰后的竞争动力。

Method: 提出REWIND（Realized Elimination Wins Determinant）指标，根据球队在事后淘汰日期后获得的胜场数进行排名。Ex Post Gold方案使用REWIND指标创建简单系统，其中事后淘汰日期的不确定性使球队无法准确预测激励转换的时间点。

Result: REWIND机制削弱了战略性输球的激励，同时保持了淘汰后的竞争动力。事后淘汰日期通常比其他方案提出的转折点更早，因此球队有更长时间的最佳策略是赢球。在大多数赛季中，每支球队都有激励至少赢下一半的比赛。

Conclusion: Ex Post Gold方案通过引入事后淘汰日期的不确定性，有效减少了选秀抽签中的摆烂行为，创造了更公平的竞争环境，激励球队在整个赛季保持竞争力。

Abstract: Many recent proposals for reducing tanking in draft lotteries share a common structure: losses improve draft position early in the season while wins improve draft position later. While such systems improve late-season incentives, they retain a predictable pivot point that tanking teams can exploit strategically. This paper proposes a simple modification that introduces uncertainty into the timing of the incentive switch. The proposed metric, the \emph{Realized Elimination Wins Determinant} (REWIND), ranks teams according to the number of wins obtained after their ex post elimination date, which makes this a variation of the Gold Plan. Because the ex post elimination date cannot be known with certainty during the season, the mechanism weakens incentives for strategic losing while preserving incentives for competitive effort after elimination. Moreover, the ex post elimination date is typically earlier than other proposed pivot points, so there is a longer period where a tanking team's best strategy is to win. The Ex Post Gold plan uses the REWIND metric to create a simple system where every team will be incentivized to win at least half of their games in most seasons.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [Speculating Experts Accelerates Inference for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19289)
*Vivan Madan,Prajwal Singhania,Abhinav Bhatele,Tom Goldstein,Ashwinee Panda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出专家预取方案，利用模型内部表示预测未来专家，实现CPU-GPU传输与计算重叠，减少MoE模型在内存受限推理中的性能瓶颈


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型在内存受限推理场景中，专家权重需要卸载到CPU，导致CPU-GPU传输成为解码性能瓶颈，需要解决这一内存传输延迟问题

Method: 提出专家预取方案，利用当前计算的内部模型表示推测未来专家，实现内存传输与计算重叠；对于推测执行效果不佳的情况，采用轻量级估计器提高专家预测命中率

Result: 在多种MoE架构上证明内部表示能可靠预测未来专家；推测执行通常能保持下游任务准确性；集成到优化推理引擎后，相比按需加载专家，每输出token时间减少高达14%

Conclusion: 专家预取方案能有效减少MoE模型在内存受限推理中的CPU-GPU传输瓶颈，通过推测执行和轻量级估计器实现性能提升，代码已开源

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have gained popularity as a means of scaling the capacity of large language models (LLMs) while maintaining sparse activations and reduced per-token compute. However, in memory-constrained inference settings, expert weights must be offloaded to CPU, creating a performance bottleneck from CPU-GPU transfers during decoding. We propose an expert prefetching scheme that leverages currently computed internal model representations to speculate future experts, enabling memory transfers to overlap with computation. Across multiple MoE architectures, we demonstrate that future experts can be reliably predicted by these internal representations. We also demonstrate that executing speculated experts generally maintains downstream task accuracy, thus preserving more effective compute-memory overlap by eliminating the need to re-fetch true router-selected experts. Integrated into an optimized inference engine, our approach achieves up to 14\% reduction in time per output token (TPOT) over on-demand loading of experts from CPU memory. For MoEs where speculative execution alone yields suboptimal accuracy, we further examine lightweight estimators that improve expert prediction hit rates, thereby reducing performance degradation. Our code is released in open-source at https://github.com/axonn-ai/yalis/tree/offload_prefetch.

</details>


### [94] [A Visualization for Comparative Analysis of Regression Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19291)
*Nassime Mountasir,Baptiste Lafabregue,Bruno Albert,Nicolas Lachiche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新的回归模型性能可视化方法，通过二维残差空间、马氏距离和百分位色彩映射来更全面地评估和比较模型性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统回归性能指标（如MAE、RMSE、R²）虽然广泛使用，但信息聚合过多，无法揭示误差分布细节和模型间的复杂关系，需要更全面的可视化方法来深入理解模型性能差异

Method: 构建三维可视化框架：1) 在二维空间中同时展示两个模型的残差；2) 使用马氏距离考虑数据相关性和尺度差异；3) 采用百分位色彩映射可视化误差分布，突出密集区域和异常值

Result: 该方法提供了比传统聚合指标更详细全面的模型性能视图，能够揭示传统方法可能掩盖的模式，增强了对回归模型性能差异和误差分布的理解

Conclusion: 提出的可视化方法通过图形化表示误差分布及其相关性，为回归模型性能评估和比较提供了更深入的分析工具，有助于发现传统指标无法揭示的性能特征

Abstract: As regression is a widely studied problem, many methods have been proposed to solve it, each of them often requiring setting different hyper-parameters. Therefore, selecting the proper method for a given application may be very difficult and relies on comparing their performances. Performance is usually measured using various metrics such as Mean Absolute Error (MAE), Root Mean Squared Error (RMSE), or R-squared (R${}^2$). These metrics provide a numerical summary of predictive accuracy by quantifying the difference between predicted and actual values. However, while these metrics are widely used in the literature for summarizing model performance and useful to distinguish between models performing poorly and well, they often aggregate too much information. This article addresses these limitations by introducing a novel visualization approach that highlights key aspects of regression model performance. The proposed method builds upon three main contributions: (1) considering the residuals in a 2D space, which allows for simultaneous evaluation of errors from two models, (2) leveraging the Mahalanobis distance to account for correlations and differences in scale within the data, and (3) employing a colormap to visualize the percentile-based distribution of errors, making it easier to identify dense regions and outliers. By graphically representing the distribution of errors and their correlations, this approach provides a more detailed and comprehensive view of model performance, enabling users to uncover patterns that traditional aggregate metrics may obscure. The proposed visualization method facilitates a deeper understanding of regression model performance differences and error distributions, enhancing the evaluation and comparison process.

</details>


### [95] [Maximizing mutual information between user-contexts and responses improve LLM personalization with no additional data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19294)
*Hyunji Nam,Haoran Li,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MIPO是一种通过对比数据增强构建偏好对的自改进框架，无需外部监督即可提升LLM性能，在个性化和推理任务上均有显著改进


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练方法严重依赖人工标注数据或外部验证器，高质量数据获取成本高昂，且真正智能远超可验证任务范畴，需要无需外部监督的自改进框架

Method: 提出互信息偏好优化(MIPO)：通过基于正确提示生成正响应、基于随机无关提示生成负响应来构建对比偏好对，使用DPO学习这些配对数据，最大化提示与模型响应之间的点对点条件互信息

Result: 在Llama和Qwen-Instruct模型上的实验表明：用于最大化用户上下文与响应间互信息时，MIPO作为个性化技术，在真实用户数据集上比强基线提升3-40%；在数学和多选题任务上，无需额外数据或人工监督即可获得1-18%提升

Conclusion: MIPO展示了无需外部监督的自改进潜力，为LLM自我提升提供了有前景的方向，表明通过最大化互信息可以实现模型在个性化和推理任务上的自主改进

Abstract: While post-training has successfully improved large language models (LLMs) across a variety of domains, these gains heavily rely on human-labeled data or external verifiers. Existing data has already been exploited, and new high-quality data is expensive to collect. More fundamentally, true intelligence goes far beyond tasks that are easily verifiable. Therefore, we need self-improvement frameworks that allow models to improve without external oversight. We propose *Mutual Information Preference Optimization (MIPO)*, a contrastive data augmentation method that constructs preference pairs by generating a positive response conditioning on the correct prompt, and a negative response by conditioning on a random, unrelated prompt. We show that using Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to learn from this paired data maximizes pointwise conditional mutual information (MI) (under the base LLM) between prompts and model responses. Empirical results with various-sized Llama- and Qwen-Instruct models show that when used to maximize MI between user context and response, MIPO provides an effective personalization technique, achieving 3-40% improvements on personalization tasks using real-user datasets compared to strong baselines. Surprisingly, MIPO can also be applied to improve performance on math and multiple-choice problems, yielding 1-18% **without any additional data or human supervision**. These results suggest a promising direction for self-improvement.

</details>


### [96] [BrainSCL: Subtype-Guided Contrastive Learning for Brain Disorder Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19295)
*Xiaolong Li,Guiliang Guo,Guangqi Wen,Peng Cao,Jinzhu Yang,Honglin Wu,Xiaoli Liu,Fei Wang,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种亚型引导的对比学习框架，通过建模患者异质性作为潜在亚型，结合临床文本和BOLD信号构建多视图表征，使用原型注意力机制捕获亚型特异性连接模式，提升精神障碍分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精神障碍人群存在显著的异质性，这给对比学习中正样本对的构建带来挑战。需要一种能够建模患者异质性并将其作为结构先验来指导判别性表征学习的方法。

Method: 1) 结合临床文本和从BOLD信号自适应学习的图结构构建多视图表征；2) 通过无监督谱聚类发现潜在亚型；3) 提出双级注意力机制构建原型以捕获稳定的亚型特异性连接模式；4) 提出亚型引导的对比学习策略，将样本拉向其亚型原型图，增强亚型内一致性。

Result: 在重度抑郁症、双相情感障碍和自闭症谱系障碍上评估，实验结果表明亚型原型图在指导对比学习方面有效，所提方法优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 提出的亚型引导对比学习框架能够有效建模精神障碍患者的异质性，通过将亚型作为结构先验指导表征学习，显著提升了模型性能，为精神障碍的精准诊断提供了新思路。

Abstract: Mental disorder populations exhibit pronounced heterogeneity -- that is, the significant differences between samples -- poses a significant challenge to the definition of positive pairs in contrastive learning. To address this, we propose a subtype-guided contrastive learning framework that models patient heterogeneity as latent subtypes and incorporates them as structural priors to guide discriminative representation learning. Specifically, we construct multi-view representations by combining patients' clinical text with graph structure adaptively learned from BOLD signals, to uncover latent subtypes via unsupervised spectral clustering. A dual-level attention mechanism is proposed to construct prototypes for capturing stable subtype-specific connectivity patterns. We further propose a subtype-guided contrastive learning strategy that pulls samples toward their subtype prototype graph, reinforcing intra-subtype consistency for providing effective supervisory signals to improve model performance. We evaluate our method on Major Depressive Disorder (MDD), Bipolar Disorder (BD), and Autism Spectrum Disorders (ASD). Experimental results confirm the effectiveness of subtype prototype graphs in guiding contrastive learning and demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms state-of-the-art approaches. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/BrainSCL-06D7.

</details>


### [97] [TTQ: Activation-Aware Test-Time Quantization to Accelerate LLM Inference On The Fly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19296)
*Toshiaki Koike-Akino,Jing Liu,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出测试时量化框架，在推理时动态压缩大型基础模型，解决传统量化方法因依赖校准数据而面临的领域偏移问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型基础模型计算需求巨大，现有无需重训练的激活感知压缩技术高度依赖校准数据，在未见下游任务上可能面临领域偏移问题

Method: 提出测试时量化框架，通过高效的在线校准，在推理时即时进行激活感知量化，能够适应每个提示而不受下游任务限制

Result: 实验表明TTQ在量化性能上优于现有最先进基线方法，同时实现推理加速

Conclusion: 测试时量化框架有效解决了传统量化方法的领域偏移问题，能够在推理时动态适应不同下游任务，实现高效压缩和性能提升

Abstract: To tackle the huge computational demand of large foundation models, activation-aware compression techniques without retraining have been introduced. However, since these methods highly rely on calibration data, domain shift issues may arise for unseen downstream tasks. We propose a test-time quantization (TTQ) framework which compresses large models on the fly at inference time to resolve this issue. With an efficient online calibration, instant activation-aware quantization can adapt every prompt regardless of the downstream tasks, yet achieving inference speedup. Several experiments demonstrate that TTQ can improve the quantization performance over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [98] [CLaRE-ty Amid Chaos: Quantifying Representational Entanglement to Predict Ripple Effects in LLM Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19297)
*Manit Baser,Alperen Yildiz,Dinil Mon Divakaran,Mohan Gurusamy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CLaRE是一种轻量级的表示层技术，用于识别LLM模型编辑中的涟漪效应，通过前向激活量化事实间的纠缠关系，无需反向传播计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的静态知识表示会随时间过时或错误，模型编辑技术虽然能修改事实关联，但会产生不可预测的涟漪效应（传播到隐藏空间的无意行为变化），需要有效方法来识别这些效应。

Method: CLaRE使用单一中间层的前向激活来量化事实间的纠缠关系，避免了昂贵的反向传播计算。作者构建了包含11,427个事实的语料库，计算大规模纠缠图来捕捉局部编辑在表示空间中的传播。

Result: 与基线方法相比，CLaRE在Spearman相关性上平均提升62.2%，速度提升2.74倍，峰值GPU内存使用减少2.85倍，存储需求也显著降低。纠缠图支持更强的模型编辑保护集、审计追踪、高效红队测试和可扩展的编辑后评估。

Conclusion: CLaRE提供了一种高效、轻量级的方法来识别模型编辑中的涟漪效应，通过前向激活分析事实纠缠关系，为模型编辑的安全性和可预测性提供了重要工具。

Abstract: The static knowledge representations of large language models (LLMs) inevitably become outdated or incorrect over time. While model-editing techniques offer a promising solution by modifying a model's factual associations, they often produce unpredictable ripple effects, which are unintended behavioral changes that propagate even to the hidden space. In this work, we introduce CLaRE, a lightweight representation-level technique to identify where these ripple effects may occur. Unlike prior gradient-based methods, CLaRE quantifies entanglement between facts using forward activations from a single intermediate layer, avoiding costly backward passes. To enable systematic study, we prepare and analyse a corpus of 11,427 facts drawn from three existing datasets. Using CLaRE, we compute large-scale entanglement graphs of this corpus for multiple models, capturing how local edits propagate through representational space. These graphs enable stronger preservation sets for model editing, audit trails, efficient red-teaming, and scalable post-edit evaluation. In comparison to baselines, CLaRE achieves an average of 62.2% improvement in Spearman correlation with ripple effects while being $2.74\times$ faster, and using $2.85\times$ less peak GPU memory. Besides, CLaRE requires only a fraction of the storage needed by the baselines to compute and preserve fact representations. Our entanglement graphs and corpus are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CLaRE-488E.

</details>


### [99] [PRIME-CVD: A Parametrically Rendered Informatics Medical Environment for Education in Cardiovascular Risk Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19299)
*Nicholas I-Hsien Kuo,Marzia Hoque Tania,Blanca Gallego,Louisa Jorm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRIME-CVD是一个参数化渲染的医学信息环境，提供两个公开可用的合成数据集（5万成人队列），用于心血管疾病预防的教学和方法开发，避免了真实患者数据的隐私风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于隐私、治理和重新识别风险，患者级电子病历数据很少用于教学或方法开发，这限制了心血管风险建模的可重复性、透明性和实践培训。

Method: 基于用户指定的因果有向无环图，使用澳大利亚人口统计数据和已发表的流行病学效应估计进行参数化，生成完全合成的数据集，而非基于真实患者数据或生成模型。

Result: 创建了两个数据集：数据资产1提供干净、分析就绪的队列，适合探索性分析、分层和生存建模；数据资产2将同一队列重构为关系型、EMR风格的数据库，具有真实的结构和词汇异质性。

Conclusion: PRIME-CVD支持数据清理、协调、因果推理和政策相关风险建模的教学，同时保持真实的亚组不平衡和风险梯度，确保可忽略的披露风险，促进可重复研究和可扩展的医学教育。

Abstract: In recent years, progress in medical informatics and machine learning has been accelerated by the availability of openly accessible benchmark datasets. However, patient-level electronic medical record (EMR) data are rarely available for teaching or methodological development due to privacy, governance, and re-identification risks. This has limited reproducibility, transparency, and hands-on training in cardiovascular risk modelling. Here we introduce PRIME-CVD, a parametrically rendered informatics medical environment designed explicitly for medical education. PRIME-CVD comprises two openly accessible synthetic data assets representing a cohort of 50,000 adults undergoing primary prevention for cardiovascular disease. The datasets are generated entirely from a user-specified causal directed acyclic graph parameterised using publicly available Australian population statistics and published epidemiologic effect estimates, rather than from patient-level EMR data or trained generative models. Data Asset 1 provides a clean, analysis-ready cohort suitable for exploratory analysis, stratification, and survival modelling, while Data Asset 2 restructures the same cohort into a relational, EMR-style database with realistic structural and lexical heterogeneity. Together, these assets enable instruction in data cleaning, harmonisation, causal reasoning, and policy-relevant risk modelling without exposing sensitive information. Because all individuals and events are generated de novo, PRIME-CVD preserves realistic subgroup imbalance and risk gradients while ensuring negligible disclosure risk. PRIME-CVD is released under a Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 licence to support reproducible research and scalable medical education.

</details>


### [100] [Parameter-Efficient Token Embedding Editing for Clinical Class-Level Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19302)
*Iyad Ait Hou,Shrenik Borad,Harsh Sharma,Pooja Srinivasan,Rebecca Hwa,Aya Zirikly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STEU是一种参数高效的临床语言模型遗忘方法，仅更新PMI选择的token嵌入和小型分类头，实现目标类别的行为级遗忘，同时保持模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床语言模型需要满足隐私法规和机构政策要求，能够在部署后删除敏感信息而无需从头重新训练。实际应用中需要在有效遗忘目标信息、保持模型效用和最小化参数修改之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出稀疏token嵌入遗忘（STEU）方法，这是一种参数高效的行为级类别遗忘方法。该方法仅更新通过点互信息（PMI）选择的token嵌入以及一个小型分类头，同时保持所有编码器层冻结不变。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV、MIMIC-III和eICU数据集上使用BioClinicalBERT、BERT-base和DistilBERT进行实验，STEU始终能够抑制目标类别，同时很大程度上保留原有任务性能。在主要MIMIC-IV设置中，STEU实现了近乎完全的遗忘（遗忘F1 = 0.0004），同时保持有竞争力的保留效用（保留平均F1 = 0.4766），仅修改了0.19%的模型参数。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过稀疏嵌入编辑而不修改更深层的编码器表示，可以实现有针对性的行为级遗忘。这种方法为临床语言模型提供了高效、实用的遗忘解决方案。

Abstract: Machine unlearning is increasingly important for clinical language models, where privacy regulations and institutional policies may require removing sensitive information from deployed systems without retraining from scratch. In practice, deletion requests must balance effective forgetting of targeted information with preservation of model utility and minimal parameter modification. We introduce Sparse Token Embedding Unlearning (STEU), a parameter-efficient method for behavioral class-level unlearning that updates only PMI-selected token embeddings together with a small classifier head while keeping all encoder layers frozen. Across experiments on MIMIC-IV, MIMIC-III, and eICU using BioClinicalBERT, BERT-base, and DistilBERT, STEU consistently suppresses the target class while largely preserving retained task performance. In the primary MIMIC-IV setting, STEU achieves near-complete forgetting (forget F1 = 0.0004) while maintaining competitive retained utility (retain avg F1 = 0.4766) after modifying only 0.19\% of model parameters. These results suggest that targeted behavioral unlearning can be achieved through sparse embedding edits without modifying deeper encoder representations.

</details>


### [101] [LeWorldModel: Stable End-to-End Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture from Pixels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19312)
*Lucas Maes,Quentin Le Lidec,Damien Scieur,Yann LeCun,Randall Balestriero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeWorldModel (LeWM) 是一种新型联合嵌入预测架构，仅使用两个损失项就能从原始像素稳定端到端训练，无需复杂多损失项、指数移动平均、预训练编码器或辅助监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA方法在紧凑潜在空间中学习世界模型时存在脆弱性，依赖复杂的多损失项、指数移动平均、预训练编码器或辅助监督来避免表示崩溃，需要简化并提高稳定性。

Method: LeWM仅使用两个损失项：下一个嵌入预测损失和强制潜在嵌入服从高斯分布的规范化器，将可调损失超参数从6个减少到1个，模型约1500万参数可在单GPU上几小时训练完成。

Result: LeWM在2D和3D控制任务中保持竞争力，规划速度比基于基础模型的世界模型快达48倍，潜在空间编码有意义的物理结构，并能可靠检测物理上不可信的事件。

Conclusion: LeWM展示了仅用两个损失项就能实现稳定端到端训练的可行性，为紧凑潜在空间中的世界模型学习提供了更简单、更高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) offer a compelling framework for learning world models in compact latent spaces, yet existing methods remain fragile, relying on complex multi-term losses, exponential moving averages, pre-trained encoders, or auxiliary supervision to avoid representation collapse. In this work, we introduce LeWorldModel (LeWM), the first JEPA that trains stably end-to-end from raw pixels using only two loss terms: a next-embedding prediction loss and a regularizer enforcing Gaussian-distributed latent embeddings. This reduces tunable loss hyperparameters from six to one compared to the only existing end-to-end alternative. With ~15M parameters trainable on a single GPU in a few hours, LeWM plans up to 48x faster than foundation-model-based world models while remaining competitive across diverse 2D and 3D control tasks. Beyond control, we show that LeWM's latent space encodes meaningful physical structure through probing of physical quantities. Surprise evaluation confirms that the model reliably detects physically implausible events.

</details>


### [102] [DPxFin: Adaptive Differential Privacy for Anti-Money Laundering Detection via Reputation-Weighted Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19314)
*Renuga Kanagavelu,Manjil Nepal,Ning Peiyan,Cai Kangning,Xu Jiming,Fei Gao,Yong Liu,Goh Siow Mong Rick,Qingsong Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DPxFin：一种新颖的联邦学习框架，通过信誉引导的自适应差分隐私来增强金融反洗钱任务的隐私保护，在保持模型效用的同时有效防止表格数据泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代金融系统中，反洗钱面临数据隐私担忧和复杂欺诈模式的挑战。联邦学习虽能避免数据共享，但在表格数据（如金融数据）上存在隐私泄露风险。需要一种既能保护隐私又能维持模型效用的解决方案。

Method: 提出DPxFin框架，集成信誉引导的自适应差分隐私。通过评估本地模型与全局模型的对齐度计算客户端信誉，基于信誉动态分配差分隐私噪声：高信誉客户端获得较低噪声以放大其可信贡献，低信誉客户端获得较强噪声以降低风险。

Result: 在反洗钱数据集上（IID和非IID设置）使用MLP进行验证。实验表明，相比传统联邦学习和固定噪声差分隐私基线，DPxFin在准确性和隐私性之间取得了更理想的权衡，性能改进虽适度但一致。框架能抵御表格数据泄露攻击，证明其在真实金融条件下的有效性。

Conclusion: DPxFin通过信誉引导的自适应差分隐私机制，为金融反洗钱任务提供了一种有效的隐私保护解决方案，在保护敏感表格数据的同时保持了模型性能，适用于实际金融应用场景。

Abstract: In the modern financial system, combating money laundering is a critical challenge complicated by data privacy concerns and increasingly complex fraud transaction patterns. Although federated learning (FL) is a promising problem-solving approach as it allows institutions to train their models without sharing their data, it has the drawback of being prone to privacy leakage, specifically in tabular data forms like financial data. To address this, we propose DPxFin, a novel federated framework that integrates reputation-guided adaptive differential privacy. Our approach computes client reputation by evaluating the alignment between locally trained models and the global model. Based on this reputation, we dynamically assign differential privacy noise to client updates, enhancing privacy while maintaining overall model utility. Clients with higher reputations receive lower noise to amplify their trustworthy contributions, while low-reputation clients are allocated stronger noise to mitigate risk. We validate DPxFin on the Anti-Money Laundering (AML) dataset under both IID and non-IID settings using Multi Layer Perceptron (MLP). Experimental analysis established that our approach has a more desirable trade-off between accuracy and privacy than those of traditional FL and fixed-noise Differential Privacy (DP) baselines, where performance improvements were consistent, even though on a modest scale. Moreover, DPxFin does withstand tabular data leakage attacks, proving its effectiveness under real-world financial conditions.

</details>


### [103] [Deep Hilbert--Galerkin Methods for Infinite-Dimensional PDEs and Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19463)
*Samuel N. Cohen,Filippo de Feo,Jackson Hebner,Justin Sirignano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了基于深度学习的全非线性二阶PDE在可分希尔伯特空间上的近似方法，通过希尔伯特-伽辽金神经算子参数化解，证明了适用于此类问题的通用逼近定理，并开发了数值训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决可分希尔伯特空间上的全非线性二阶PDE（如无限维控制的HJB方程）的近似问题。传统方法通常将PDE投影到有限维空间，而本文首次提出在整个希尔伯特空间上最小化PDE残差的L²范数的方法。

Method: 1. 提出希尔伯特-伽辽金神经算子（HGNOs）参数化PDE解；2. 证明适用于希尔伯特空间函数及其一阶、二阶Fréchet导数的通用逼近定理；3. 开发深度希尔伯特-伽辽金方法和希尔伯特演员-评论家（强化学习）方法；4. 在整个希尔伯特空间上最小化PDE残差的L²范数进行训练。

Result: 1. 首次证明了适用于希尔伯特空间全非线性二阶PDE的通用逼近定理；2. 开发了首个在整个希尔伯特空间上训练的方法；3. 成功数值求解了与确定性/随机热方程和Burgers方程最优控制相关的Kolmogorov和HJB PDE示例；4. 展示了深度学习方法的潜力。

Conclusion: 该研究为希尔伯特空间上的全非线性二阶PDE提供了基于深度学习的有效近似框架，通过HGNOs和创新的训练方法，成功解决了无限维控制等问题，展示了在物理、随机系统等应用领域的广阔前景。

Abstract: We develop deep learning-based approximation methods for fully nonlinear second-order PDEs on separable Hilbert spaces, such as HJB equations for infinite-dimensional control, by parameterizing solutions via Hilbert--Galerkin Neural Operators (HGNOs). We prove the first Universal Approximation Theorems (UATs) which are sufficiently powerful to address these problems, based on novel topologies for Hessian terms and corresponding novel continuity assumptions on the fully nonlinear operator. These topologies are non-sequential and non-metrizable, making the problem delicate. In particular, we prove UATs for functions on Hilbert spaces, together with their Fréchet derivatives up to second order, and for unbounded operators applied to the first derivative, ensuring that HGNOs are able to approximate all the PDE terms. For control problems, we further prove UATs for optimal feedback controls in terms of our approximating value function HGNO.
  We develop numerical training methods, which we call Deep Hilbert--Galerkin and Hilbert Actor-Critic (reinforcement learning) Methods, for these problems by minimizing the $L^2_μ(H)$-norm of the residual of the PDE on the whole Hilbert space, not just a projected PDE to finite dimensions. This is the first paper to propose such an approach. The models considered arise in many applied sciences, such as functional differential equations in physics and Kolmogorov and HJB PDEs related to controlled PDEs, SPDEs, path-dependent systems, partially observed stochastic systems, and mean-field SDEs. We numerically solve examples of Kolmogorov and HJB PDEs related to the optimal control of deterministic and stochastic heat and Burgers' equations, demonstrating the promise of our deep learning-based approach.

</details>


### [104] [Global Convergence of Multiplicative Updates for the Matrix Mechanism: A Collaborative Proof with Gemini 3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19465)
*Keith Rush*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文分析了一个在正则化核范数优化中出现的固定点迭代，证明了该迭代单调收敛到全局最优解，并讨论了AI在数学证明中的实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Denisov等人提出的关于Hadamard积结构的正则化核范数优化问题中遗留的收敛性证明问题，同时探索AI在数学证明中的实际应用价值。

Method: 分析固定点迭代v←φ(v)，其中φ(v)=diag((D_v^{1/2}MD_v^{1/2})^{1/2})，证明该迭代单调收敛到J(v)=2Tr((D_v^{1/2}MD_v^{1/2})^{1/2})-∑v_i的唯一全局最优解。主要证明由Gemini 3 AI完成，经过人工修正和干预。

Result: 证明了迭代v^{(k+1)}=diag((D_{v^{(k)}}^{1/2}MD_{v^{(k)}}^{1/2})^{1/2})单调收敛到势函数J(v)的唯一全局最优解，解决了文献中的开放问题。

Conclusion: 该迭代确实收敛到全局最优解，填补了文献空白。论文同时展示了AI（特别是Gemini 3）在数学证明中的实际应用潜力，提供了使用AI进行数学研究的指导原则。

Abstract: We analyze a fixed-point iteration $v \leftarrow φ(v)$ arising in the optimization of a regularized nuclear norm objective involving the Hadamard product structure, posed in~\cite{denisov} in the context of an optimization problem over the space of algorithms in private machine learning. We prove that the iteration $v^{(k+1)} = \text{diag}((D_{v^{(k)}}^{1/2} M D_{v^{(k)}}^{1/2})^{1/2})$ converges monotonically to the unique global optimizer of the potential function $J(v) = 2 \text{Tr}((D_v^{1/2} M D_v^{1/2})^{1/2}) - \sum v_i$, closing a problem left open there.
  The bulk of this proof was provided by Gemini 3, subject to some corrections and interventions. Gemini 3 also sketched the initial version of this note. Thus, it represents as much a commentary on the practical use of AI in mathematics as it represents the closure of a small gap in the literature. As such, we include a small narrative description of the prompting process, and some resulting principles for working with AI to prove mathematics.

</details>


### [105] [A General Deep Learning Framework for Wireless Resource Allocation under Discrete Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19322)
*Yikun Wang,Yang Li,Yik-Chung Wu,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个基于支持集的深度学习框架，通过概率建模解决无线资源分配中离散变量的零梯度、约束满足和非SPSD属性等挑战


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在连续无线资源分配中表现出色，但在处理离散变量时面临三大挑战：反向传播的零梯度问题、离散变量复杂约束的强制执行困难、以及无法生成具有非相同参数相同决策属性的解

Method: 引入支持集表示离散变量，将支持集元素建模为随机变量并学习其联合概率分布。通过将联合概率分解为条件概率的乘积，顺序学习每个条件概率。使用概率分布代替硬二进制决策，通过掩码机制强制执行约束，并采用动态上下文嵌入捕获演化中的离散解

Result: 在两种代表性混合离散无线资源分配问题上的仿真结果表明，所提DL框架在系统性能和计算效率方面均优于现有基线方法

Conclusion: 提出的基于支持集的概率建模框架有效解决了深度学习处理离散变量的核心挑战，为混合离散无线资源分配问题提供了通用且高效的解决方案

Abstract: While deep learning (DL)-based methods have achieved remarkable success in continuous wireless resource allocation, efficient solutions for problems involving discrete variables remain challenging. This is primarily due to the zero-gradient issue in backpropagation, the difficulty of enforcing intricate constraints with discrete variables, and the inability in generating solutions with non-same-parameter-same-decision (non-SPSD) property. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a general DL framework by introducing the support set to represent the discrete variables. We model the elements of the support set as random variables and learn their joint probability distribution. By factorizing the joint probability as the product of conditional probabilities, each conditional probability is sequentially learned. This probabilistic modeling directly tackles all the aforementioned challenges of DL for handling discrete variables. By operating on probability distributions instead of hard binary decisions, the framework naturally avoids the zero-gradient issue. During the learning of the conditional probabilities, discrete constraints can be seamlessly enforced by masking out infeasible solutions. Moreover, with a dynamic context embedding that captures the evolving discrete solutions, the non-SPSD property is inherently provided by the proposed framework. We apply the proposed framework to two representative mixed-discrete wireless resource allocation problems: (a) joint user association and beamforming in cell-free systems, and (b) joint antenna positioning and beamforming in movable antenna-aided systems. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed DL framework consistently outperforms existing baselines in terms of both system performance and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [106] [Heavy-Tailed and Long-Range Dependent Noise in Stochastic Approximation: A Finite-Time Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19648)
*Siddharth Chandak,Anuj Yadav,Ayfer Ozgur,Nicholas Bambos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究在重尾和长程依赖噪声下的随机逼近算法，建立了首个有限时间矩界，并应用于SGD和梯度博弈


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机逼近分析通常基于鞅差或马尔可夫噪声的有限二阶矩假设，但金融、通信等实际应用中经常遇到重尾和长程依赖噪声，需要研究在这些非经典噪声模型下的随机逼近算法性能

Method: 采用噪声平均化论证来正则化噪声影响而不修改迭代过程，研究在强单调算子求根问题下的随机逼近算法，分析重尾和长程依赖噪声下的收敛性

Result: 建立了在这两种噪声设置下的首个有限时间矩界，提供了显式收敛率来量化重尾和长程依赖的影响

Conclusion: 提出的通用框架可应用于随机梯度下降和梯度博弈，数值实验验证了有限时间分析的有效性，为实际应用中处理非经典噪声提供了理论保证

Abstract: Stochastic approximation (SA) is a fundamental iterative framework with broad applications in reinforcement learning and optimization. Classical analyses typically rely on martingale difference or Markov noise with bounded second moments, but many practical settings, including finance and communications, frequently encounter heavy-tailed and long-range dependent (LRD) noise. In this work, we study SA for finding the root of a strongly monotone operator under these non-classical noise models. We establish the first finite-time moment bounds in both settings, providing explicit convergence rates that quantify the impact of heavy tails and temporal dependence. Our analysis employs a noise-averaging argument that regularizes the impact of noise without modifying the iteration. Finally, we apply our general framework to stochastic gradient descent (SGD) and gradient play, and corroborate our finite-time analysis through numerical experiments.

</details>


### [107] [Target Concept Tuning Improves Extreme Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19325)
*Shijie Ren,Xinyue Gu,Ziheng Peng,Haifan Zhang,Peisong Niu,Bo Wu,Xiting Wang,Liang Sun,Jirong Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TaCT是一个可解释的概念门控微调框架，通过选择性模型改进解决气象预报中罕见但高影响事件（如台风）的预测问题，在保持常见场景性能的同时专门针对失败案例进行模型适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习气象预报模型在罕见但高影响事件（如台风）中经常失败，因为相关数据稀缺。现有的微调方法通常面临两难选择：要么忽视这些极端事件，要么以牺牲整体性能为代价过度拟合它们。

Method: 提出TaCT框架，使用稀疏自编码器和反事实分析自动发现与失败相关的内部概念，仅在相应概念被激活时更新参数，而不是应用统一的适应策略。通过概念门控机制实现选择性模型改进。

Result: 实验显示在不同地区的台风预报中取得一致改进，且不降低其他气象变量的预测性能。识别的概念对应物理上有意义的环流模式，揭示了模型偏差并支持科学预报任务中的可信适应。

Conclusion: TaCT框架通过可解释的概念门控微调，成功解决了气象预报中罕见高影响事件的预测问题，在保持整体性能的同时专门改进失败案例，为科学预报任务提供了可信的适应方法。

Abstract: Deep learning models for meteorological forecasting often fail in rare but high-impact events such as typhoons, where relevant data is scarce. Existing fine-tuning methods typically face a trade-off between overlooking these extreme events and overfitting them at the expense of overall performance. We propose TaCT, an interpretable concept-gated fine-tuning framework that solves the aforementioned issue by selective model improvement: models are adapted specifically for failure cases while preserving performance in common scenarios. To this end, TaCT automatically discovers failure-related internal concepts using Sparse Autoencoders and counterfactual analysis, and updates parameters only when the corresponding concepts are activated, rather than applying uniform adaptation. Experiments show consistent improvements in typhoon forecasting across different regions without degrading other meteorological variables. The identified concepts correspond to physically meaningful circulation patterns, revealing model biases and supporting trustworthy adaptation in scientific forecasting tasks. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Concept-Gated-Fine-tune-62AC.

</details>


### [108] [FalconBC: Flow matching for Amortized inference of Latent-CONditioned physiologic Boundary Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19331)
*Chloe H. Choi,Alison L. Marsden,Daniele E. Schiavazzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于概率流的摊销推理框架，用于心血管建模中的边界条件调优，解决开放环路模型和血管病变解剖结构中的联合估计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动变分推理方法在两种重要场景下存在不足：1) 开放环路模型（已知平均流量和假设波形形状）；2) 受血管病变影响的解剖结构（分割影响压力或流量分配目标的可达性）。这两种情况下边界条件无法单独调优。

Method: 引入基于概率流的通用摊销推理框架，将临床目标、流入特征和患者特定解剖的点云嵌入作为条件变量或联合估计量，实现边界条件的联合后验分布估计。

Result: 在两个患者特定模型上验证了方法：1) 具有不同狭窄位置和严重程度的主动脉-髂动脉分叉；2) 冠状动脉树。证明了框架在复杂解剖条件下的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的概率流摊销推理框架能够处理心血管建模中边界条件无法单独调优的挑战性场景，为患者特异性建模提供了更通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Boundary condition tuning is a fundamental step in patient-specific cardiovascular modeling. Despite an increase in offline training cost, recent methods in data-driven variational inference can efficiently estimate the joint posterior distribution of boundary conditions, with amortization of training efforts over clinical targets. However, even the most modern approaches fall short in two important scenarios: open-loop models with known mean flow and assumed waveform shapes, and anatomies affected by vascular lesions where segmentation influences the reachability of pressure or flow split targets. In both cases, boundary conditions cannot be tuned in isolation. We introduce a general amortized inference framework based on probabilistic flow that treats clinical targets, inflow features, and point cloud embeddings of patient-specific anatomies as either conditioning variables or quantities to be jointly estimated. We demonstrate the approach on two patient-specific models: an aorto-iliac bifurcation with varying stenosis locations and severity, and a coronary arterial tree.

</details>


### [109] [DAPA: Distribution Aware Piecewise Activation Functions for On-Device Transformer Inference and Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19338)
*Maoyang Xiang,Bo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DAPA是一种针对Transformer架构的硬件友好激活函数，通过利用预激活数据分布进行非均匀分段近似，在保持性能的同时显著加速GELU计算并减少硬件资源使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性激活函数在设备端推理和训练中消耗大量硬件资源，严重影响系统性能和能效。现有方法在硬件部署时存在效率问题，需要一种既能保持性能又硬件友好的激活函数。

Method: 提出分布感知分段激活(DAPA)：1) 利用预激活数据分布进行非均匀分段近似，在高概率区域分配更细的分段；2) 使用分布加权均方误差对近似结果进行量化；3) 设计硬件友好的实现方案。

Result: HLS实现显示：DAPA将GELU计算速度提升16倍，DSP利用率降低16倍，在视觉Transformer和GPT-2模型上保持相当或更好的性能。

Conclusion: DAPA通过利用数据分布特性，实现了硬件效率与模型性能的良好平衡，为Transformer架构的硬件部署提供了高效的激活函数解决方案。

Abstract: Non-linear activation functions play a pivotal role in on-device inference and training, as they not only consume substantial hardware resources but also impose a significant impact on system performance and energy efficiency. In this work, we propose Distribution-Aware Piecewise Activation (DAPA), a differentiable and hardware-friendly activation function for Transformer architectures by exploiting the distribution of pre-activation data. DAPA employs a non-uniform piecewise approximation that allocates finer segments to high-probability regions of the distribution, improving generalizability over prior piecewise linear methods. The resulting approximation is further quantized using Distribution-Weighted Mean Square Error to reduce latency and resource utilization for hardware deployment. Our HLS implementation demonstrates that DAPA speeds up GELU computation by 16$\times$ and decreases DSP utilization by 16$\times$ while maintaining comparable or better performance across vision Transformers and GPT-2 models.

</details>


### [110] [Anatomical Heterogeneity in Transformer Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19348)
*Tomasz Wietrzykowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现Transformer语言模型的层间存在显著异质性，挑战了传统均匀计算预算训练的假设，提出了基于层重要性的差异化训练策略，能大幅降低训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Transformer语言模型采用均匀计算预算训练，隐含假设各层同质性。本研究通过实证分析挑战这一假设，探索层间解剖学异质性及其对训练效率的影响。

Method: 使用SmolLM2-135M模型（30层，1.35亿参数），采用五种诊断指标：权重可预测性(R2)、消融退化、恢复速度、权重操作鲁棒性和结构分析，发现层间异质性规律。

Result: 发现层间存在显著异质性：1）权重遵循强数学规律但非线性误差累积导致灾难性失败；2）层重要性跨越10^7范围，存在关键核心层和反层；3）恢复速度与层重要性相关；4）仅权重缩放能保持模型质量；5）基于层重要性的差异化训练可降低54%成本。

Conclusion: Transformer层间存在显著异质性，基于层重要性的差异化训练策略能显著提高训练效率，为优化语言模型训练提供了新方向。

Abstract: Current transformer language models are trained with uniform computational budgets across all layers, implicitly assuming layer homogeneity. We challenge this assumption through empirical analysis of SmolLM2-135M, a 30-layer, 135M-parameter causal language model, using five diagnostic metrics: weight predictability (R2), ablation degradation, recovery speed, weight manipulation robustness, and structural analysis. We find profound anatomical heterogeneity: (1) Layer weights follow strong mathematical regularity (R2 = 0.91) with a universal oscillatory delta pattern (correlation ~= -0.50), yet predicted weights cause catastrophic failure due to nonlinear error accumulation. (2) Layer importance spans a 10^7 range, from a critical core (L8-11, up to +63,419% PPL degradation) to anti-layers (L14, L17) whose removal improves performance. (3) Recovery speed correlates with layer importance, indicating differential training requirements. (4) Only weight scaling (alpha = 0.9) preserves model quality among five tested manipulation strategies. (5) Growth Transformer Training, allocating budget by layer importance, achieves ~54% cost reduction. A proof-of-concept experiment confirms this: 4.7x lower validation loss than uniform training at identical parameter count, while being 13% faster.

</details>


### [111] [A Mathematical Theory of Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19349)
*Bahar Taşkesen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一个学习瓶颈的数学模型，强调学习者需要先备知识才能解码教学信号，学习速度受限于先备结构可达性和目标不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI降低了信息生产成本，但信息的价值取决于下游用户能否吸收和运用。教学信号只有在学习者具备必要先备知识时才有意义，否则只是噪音。论文旨在建模这种学习者侧瓶颈。

Method: 建立数学模型，将学习者视为"心智"——具有概念先备结构的抽象学习系统。教学建模为与潜在目标的顺序通信，有效通信信道取决于学习者当前知识状态。模型推导出两个学习速度限制：先备可达性决定的结构限制和目标不确定性决定的认知限制。

Result: 模型揭示了训练和能力获取中的阈值效应：当教学时间低于目标先备深度时，额外教学无法完成教学；一旦达到该深度，完成变得可行。在异质学习者中，通用广播课程比个性化教学慢一个与学习者类型数量成线性关系的因子。

Conclusion: 学习速度受限于学习者解码信号的结构能力，而非信息生产成本。教学有效性取决于学习者先备知识与教学信号的匹配程度，这解释了为什么相同教学对不同学习者效果差异巨大，并强调了个性化教学的重要性。

Abstract: Generative AI has transformed the economics of information production, making explanations, proofs, examples, and analyses available at very low cost. Yet the value of information still depends on whether downstream users can absorb and act on it. A signal conveys meaning only to a learner with the structural capacity to decode it: an explanation that clarifies a concept for one user may be indistinguishable from noise to another who lacks the relevant prerequisites. This paper develops a mathematical model of that learner-side bottleneck. We model the learner as a mind, an abstract learning system characterized by a prerequisite structure over concepts. A mind may represent a human learner, an artificial learner such as a neural network, or any agent whose ability to interpret signals depends on previously acquired concepts. Teaching is modeled as sequential communication with a latent target. Because instructional signals are usable only when the learner has acquired the prerequisites needed to parse them, the effective communication channel depends on the learner's current state of knowledge and becomes more informative as learning progresses. The model yields two limits on the speed of learning and adoption: a structural limit determined by prerequisite reachability and an epistemic limit determined by uncertainty about the target. The framework implies threshold effects in training and capability acquisition. When the teaching horizon lies below the prerequisite depth of the target, additional instruction cannot produce successful completion of teaching; once that depth is reached, completion becomes feasible. Across heterogeneous learners, a common broadcast curriculum can be slower than personalized instruction by a factor linear in the number of learner types.

</details>


### [112] [Warm-Start Flow Matching for Guaranteed Fast Text/Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19360)
*Minyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Warm-Start FM（WS-FM）方法，通过使用轻量级生成模型产生低质量草稿样本作为流匹配算法的初始分布，从而显著减少生成高质量样本所需的时间步数，实现有保证的加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自回归LLM、基于扩散的文本/图像生成模型和流匹配算法的推理或样本生成通常非常耗时且计算需求大，主要由于大量的函数评估（对应token长度或扩散步数）。这需要大量GPU资源、时间和电力。需要一种能减少样本生成时间而不牺牲生成质量的方法。

Method: 提出Warm-Start FM（WS-FM）方法：使用计算轻量级的生成模型生成草稿样本（质量不高但生成速度快），将其作为流匹配算法的初始分布。与传统流匹配使用纯噪声（如高斯或均匀）初始分布不同，草稿样本已有一定质量，因此可以将起始时间设置为更接近结束时间而非0，从而显著减少到达目标数据分布所需的时间步数，实现有保证的加速。

Result: 在合成玩具数据和真实世界文本、图像生成任务上验证了WS-FM方法，证明该方法能在不牺牲生成样本质量的前提下，提供有保证的样本生成加速。

Conclusion: WS-FM是一种从低质量草稿样本到高质量样本的"学习精炼"生成模型，能够显著减少流匹配算法的样本生成时间，同时保持生成质量，为解决当前生成模型推理耗时问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Current auto-regressive (AR) LLMs, diffusion-based text/image generative models, and recent flow matching (FM) algorithms are capable of generating premium quality text/image samples. However, the inference or sample generation in these models is often very time-consuming and computationally demanding, mainly due to large numbers of function evaluations corresponding to the lengths of tokens or the numbers of diffusion steps. This also necessitates heavy GPU resources, time, and electricity. In this work we propose a novel solution to reduce the sample generation time of flow matching algorithms by a guaranteed speed-up factor, without sacrificing the quality of the generated samples. Our key idea is to utilize computationally lightweight generative models whose generation time is negligible compared to that of the target AR/FM models. The draft samples from a lightweight model, whose quality is not satisfactory but fast to generate, are regarded as an initial distribution for a FM algorithm. Unlike conventional usage of FM that takes a pure noise (e.g., Gaussian or uniform) initial distribution, the draft samples are already of decent quality, so we can set the starting time to be closer to the end time rather than 0 in the pure noise FM case. This will significantly reduce the number of time steps to reach the target data distribution, and the speed-up factor is guaranteed. Our idea, dubbed {\em Warm-Start FM} or WS-FM, can essentially be seen as a {\em learning-to-refine} generative model from low-quality draft samples to high-quality samples. As a proof of concept, we demonstrate the idea on some synthetic toy data as well as real-world text and image generation tasks, illustrating that our idea offers guaranteed speed-up in sample generation without sacrificing the quality of the generated samples.

</details>


### [113] [Optimizing Resource-Constrained Non-Pharmaceutical Interventions for Multi-Cluster Outbreak Control Using Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19397)
*Xueqiao Peng,Andrew Perrault*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出分层强化学习框架，解决多集群异步爆发场景下有限非药物干预资源的优化分配问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实公共卫生场景中，多个异步爆发、规模风险各异的疫情集群需要竞争有限的非药物干预资源（如检测、隔离），传统方法难以在不确定性和异质性需求下做出有效决策

Method: 将问题建模为约束性不安定多臂老虎机问题，提出分层强化学习框架：全局控制器学习连续动作成本乘子调整全局资源需求，广义局部策略估计每个集群内个体分配资源的边际价值

Result: 在基于SARS-CoV-2的智能体模拟器中，该方法在多种系统规模和检测预算下均优于RMAB启发式和启发式基线，将疫情控制效果提升20%-30%；在多达40个并发活跃集群的实验中，分层框架展现出高度可扩展性和更快的决策速度

Conclusion: 分层强化学习框架能有效解决多集群疫情爆发场景下的资源分配问题，在不确定性和异质性需求下实现更优的疫情控制效果，且具有良好的可扩展性和决策效率

Abstract: Non-pharmaceutical interventions (NPIs), such as diagnostic testing and quarantine, are crucial for controlling infectious disease outbreaks but are often constrained by limited resources, particularly in early outbreak stages. In real-world public health settings, resources must be allocated across multiple outbreak clusters that emerge asynchronously, vary in size and risk, and compete for a shared resource budget. Here, a cluster corresponds to a group of close contacts generated by a single infected index case. Thus, decisions must be made under uncertainty and heterogeneous demands, while respecting operational constraints. We formulate this problem as a constrained restless multi-armed bandit and propose a hierarchical reinforcement learning framework. A global controller learns a continuous action cost multiplier that adjusts global resource demand, while a generalized local policy estimates the marginal value of allocating resources to individuals within each cluster. We evaluate the proposed framework in a realistic agent-based simulator of SARS-CoV-2 with dynamically arriving clusters. Across a wide range of system scales and testing budgets, our method consistently outperforms RMAB-inspired and heuristic baselines, improving outbreak control effectiveness by 20%-30%. Experiments on up to 40 concurrently active clusters further demonstrate that the hierarchical framework is highly scalable and enables faster decision-making than the RMAB-inspired method.

</details>


### [114] [GeoLAN: Geometric Learning of Latent Explanatory Directions in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19460)
*Tianyu Bell Pan,Damon L. Woodard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoLAN框架通过几何轨迹视角和Kakeya猜想启发的粘性条件训练LLMs，使用KT-CW和KT-Attn正则化器提升各向同性和注意力多样性，在Gemma和Llama模型上改善几何指标并减少公平性偏差，同时保持任务准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽然性能强大但缺乏透明度，需要开发能够增强模型机制可解释性的训练方法，特别是通过几何视角来理解模型内部表示。

Method: 提出GeoLAN训练框架，将token表示视为几何轨迹，应用Kakeya猜想启发的粘性条件。开发了两个可微分正则化器：Katz-Tao Convex Wolff (KT-CW)促进各向同性，Katz-Tao Attention (KT-Attn)鼓励注意力多样性。

Result: 在Gemma-3 (1B, 4B, 12B)和Llama-3-8B上的实验表明，GeoLAN通常能保持任务准确性，同时改善几何指标并减少某些公平性偏差。这些效益在中等规模模型中最为显著。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了几何精度与性能之间的规模依赖性权衡，表明几何感知训练是增强机制可解释性的有前景方法，特别是在中等规模模型中效果最佳。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong performance, but they often lack transparency. We introduce GeoLAN, a training framework that treats token representations as geometric trajectories and applies stickiness conditions inspired by recent developments related to the Kakeya Conjecture. We have developed two differentiable regularizers, Katz-Tao Convex Wolff (KT-CW) and Katz-Tao Attention (KT-Attn), that promote isotropy and encourage diverse attention.
